# Salt-anchored parameter handoff

Full-precision values precede printed rounding in parentheses. The count beside each record gives its denominator or state basis. Relative deviations use prediction minus observation divided by observation. Deviation columns give AARD, signed bias, relative N and available N. For conditional SE, stage degrees of freedom use responsive rows minus interior rank (#420).

The 0.765 comparison uses MEA F2, the selected #413 DEA and #411 MDEA fits, and the #434 expanded AMP fit of the same slope family. The salt osmotic control replays the historical salt laws with F2 effective inputs. The AMP −150 K fit is a sensitivity. Search counts describe sampled numerical behavior. The 262.862 MPa MDEA split, original AMP S14 split, and #432/#434 old-set sensitivities remain historical.

Engine wheel and endpoint identities are in [receiver-law-verification.json](receiver-law-verification.json). Display rebinding belongs to #429.

Historical sensitivities and certificates are documented in the [#432 handoff](../../../round_3/manuscript-handoff.md) and [#434 handoff](../../../round_5/manuscript-handoff.md).
